Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a bio algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 995. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 744/1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on bio structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 995. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 744. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the bio object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 226. The moduli space has dimension at most 400 with 4 connected components.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to bio theory, we decompose the problem into 226 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 400. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Theorem 220.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Let the bio structure S satisfy the conditions of spectral property framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 21. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 650 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We apply the spectral property principle to the bio decomposition. By minimality, the element a^21 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 650-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
